- [203](203-nphies-screen-shape.md) — The shape of the screens: four modals become what? · **done 2026-07-31** · map 196 · blocked by: 197, 199 · prototype · **HITL** · **six routes in one new area `features/nphies/`** — two lists, each with a detail + a form route. Seam = **route transition carrying an id** (`/nphies/authorizations/new?from=<eligId>`), because the auth is often raised days after the check: WPF's *carried object* becomes a fetch by id. Items land on an **inline add-row** that prices in place; **no modal opens anywhere in the flow**. The eight visibility booleans need **no replacement** — 199/200 dissolved them and `ViewOnly` was just one dialog doing duty as both form and detail; one checkbox + one conditional block survive. Principal diagnosis = **a radio in the row** (uniqueness structural), **morphology appears with its cause**. 🚩 Two corrections of record: **`Failed` = NPHIES refused on validation before the payer saw it** ⇒ a *form* state fixed in place, and **Retry belongs to `Pending`, not `Failed`** (`RetryAuth` re-POSTs the stored JSON verbatim). Acts state-driven, reason on hover. Lists default to **last 7 days as a removable chip** — what actually tames `Take(20000)`. **No browser polling**: the service's own `PollRequestWorker` polls NPHIES every 15 s, so Pending resolves itself; manual refresh only. Surfaced 207 · [prototype](assets/196-nphies/screen-shape-prototype.html)
- [204](204-nphies-the-estimate.md) — The estimate · **done 2026-08-01** · map 196 · blocked by: — · grilling · **HITL** · **35–46 developer-days engineering-complete, two teams** — ~15–20 server (SIS.Api) + ~19–25 web (oms-react); one dev end to end 7–9 weeks, two in parallel **≈ 5 weeks elapsed**. **Zero Nphies-service days** — not one ticket found work for the service team, and the money term the map opened calling "a multiple" closed as **three session verbs and one column**, both its candidate server changes priced at zero. Riskiest by far is 208's **engine-session surface at 8–12 d (could be 6, could be 18)** — a *parallel* build, since the call-centre services are doc-type-aware but call-centre-**bound**; a ~2-day de-risking spike was priced and declined. Staging `:8077` is a **schedule dependency, not an effort figure**. QA/UAT/deploy deliberately excluded (a 30–50% tail, quote it as a tail not a discount); View JSON, camera capture and before/after audit on the two line-meta codes priced and left out. Requester's four rulings: per-team + total, engineering-complete only, final-value-only audit accepted, session term carried as a range. **Spec not written here** — `/to-spec` against 196 is the map's closing act and its own session
- [205](205-nphies-who-computes-the-money.md) — Who computes the money on a web-raised authorization line · **done 2026-08-01** · map 196 · blocked by: — · grilling · **HITL** · graduated from fog by 197, shrunk by 208 · **nobody computes money on the web — the engine computes all of it and the agent supplies five inputs**: header rates, header paid-outside, line qty, line Max Coverage, line Days Supply (+ Selection Reason, a code not an amount). **Q3 dissolves at zero cost** — `InsuranceItemCategory` rides the engine line and `ResolveDeductibleGroupForLine` already maps category→G1/G2/G3 server-side, so 197's picker change is **priced at zero and dropped**. 🚩 **`DeductibleGroupName` IS `InsuranceItemCategory`** (same value, two names) — Q3 and a fifth of Q4 were one question. **`Factor` is omitted** — the service overwrites it (`Amount/ExtendedPrice`). **`MaxCoverage`/`DeductibleG`/`DeductibleGroupName` never reach NPHIES** — stored on `NAuthLine`, read back by the *dispensing till*; only `ActualPatientShare` is adjudicated. 208's override list was short by three: Max Coverage (editable cell, via `MaxPayerShare`), Selection Reason (WPF rule exactly — disabled on `Generic` only), Days Supply (both levels, **one 1–100 rule** replacing WPF's 180/90/100, deleting the submit-time sweep). Paid-outside **included** and **persisted** — new `PaidAmount` column on `PosTransactionDeductibleGroup`, because a stored cap can't distinguish 300 from 500-less-200. **Cost: 3 session verbs (8→11) + 1 column**; the auth header was already modelled on the transaction (migration 015). Audit is complete for money, **final-value-only** for `DaysSupply`/`SelectionReason`. 🚩 3 defects reported not fixed: div-by-zero at `AuthService.cs:450`, stray-semicolon parse in both `NphiesDeductibleManager` copies, SIS.Pos silently ignoring `MaxPayerShare <= 0`
- [206](206-nphies-does-the-service-check-the-money.md) — Does the Nphies service check the money it is sent · **done 2026-08-01** · map 196 · blocked by: — · task · **AFK in the end** · **no — not one guard touches an amount**, and the probe was never needed: resolved from the service's own source at `C:\Work\DMSCO\nphies\Service\NphiesService`. `Auth/Auth` is a **transcription layer** — copies every money field onto `NAuthLine` (`AuthService.cs:423-448`), rounds seven to 2dp away-from-zero, recomputes exactly one (`Factor = Amount/ExtendedPrice`, `:450`), overrides `SelectionReason` from the item master, POSTs. **What the caller sends is what the national exchange sees.** A wrong `ExtendedPrice` is accepted **silently** (never leaves the service; only skews the sent `Factor` ⇒ a wrong factor on a live claim, not a rejection); a header/line deductible mismatch is **unobservable** — `DeductibleG*`/`MaxCoverage` have no NPHIES mapping and the outbound `Claim` carries **no `Total`**. 🚩 **The case that could have cut the estimate does the opposite**: zeroed money **throws div-by-zero** at `:450`, caught blanket at `:743` ⇒ `Success=false` + an orphan `NAuth` header with no lines — so real money is required from day one and **205's engine-computes-everything becomes load-bearing**, no downstream second opinion existing. Adds **one required guard** (SIS.Api refuses `ExtendedPrice = 0`) and **one rounding rule** (`ActualPatientShare` is the only *adjudicated* field and the service does **not** round it — web rounds 2dp, parity with the till). NPHIES-side validation stays unprobed (staging `:8077` unreachable, prod `:8065` off-limits) but is **no longer a risk** — the production formula is empirically valid, so the web reproduces it rather than discovering it; a violation is 203's `Failed`. Incidental for 207: **`GET auth/AuthJson/{id}` exists** (`AuthController.cs:590`) · [findings](assets/196-nphies/service-money-checks.md)
- [208](208-nphies-the-auth-is-an-engine-document.md) — The authorization is an engine document, not a form post · **done 2026-07-31** · map 196 · blocked by: — · grilling · **HITL** · 🚩 **premise-changing** · **yes — the web drives a live `NphiesAuth` engine transaction like the till; the screen is a SESSION, not a form** (open on entry → `ScanAsync`/`ChangeQty`/`VoidLine` → `StartAsync`→POST→`MarkSubmittedAsync(authId)`; `Abandon` on leaving). Reason is the owner's own from spec 301: audit **what the engine landed vs what the agent changed** — "added then voided" can't be rebuilt from a payload of survivors. Shift-less per ADR-0001/0005 (a browser is the call-centre-device case, path proven). **A parallel Nphies session, ~8 verbs vs call centre's 25** — `CallCenter*` is doc-type-aware but call-centre-**bound** (guard at `CallCenterEngineSession.cs:103`; id hard-coded `:336/:373/:413/:500`), so the recipe is reused, not the code. Agent overrides **header deductible rates + line qty + void**, never an item swap / price / discount ⇒ **203's rate block becomes EDITABLE**. **Duplicate item refused at the SCAN** (WPF's submit-time `ValidateDuplicateItems` moved forward). **No resumable drafts** — abandon + sweeper, because 249's OPEN-claim litter is what spec 301 retired. 🚩 **Corrects 200: the acting store IS the pricing plant**, bound once at open (`CallCenterEngineSession.cs:491-493`) — invisible in the payload, decisive in the money; no store↔provider mapping exists. Shrinks **205** to its picker/fields half and **unblocks** it; grows **198** by the session surface; sharpens **207**
- [209](209-nphies-web-spec.md) — **Nphies on the web: eligibility checks and prior authorizations** · **ready** · spec · from map 196 (all 12 tickets) · **the map's destination, reached** — consumable by `/to-tickets`. Two screens in a new `features/nphies/` area, six routes, two i18n namespaces; the auth form is a **live engine session, not a form post** (11 verbs), the acting store is the pricing plant, nobody computes money in the browser. Four `/to-spec` rulings: **pure vitest + Playwright drives** (RTL still not bootstrapped, matching the call-centre console); the call-centre **session guard graduates to `core/`** and goes first, alone, since features may not import features; the **SIS.Api work is spec'd as a contract but not ticketed here** (~15–20 of the 35–46 days is another team's); **one spec**, not two. 90 user stories. Suggested slice order: core guard → eligibility list as the area tracer → check + detail → auth list + detail (no session needed) → the auth form last, since it alone blocks on the server's session verbs
- [207](207-nphies-reopening-a-refused-request.md) — Reopening a refused request: what can the form be prefilled from? · **done 2026-08-01** · map 196 · blocked by: — · research · **AFK** · surfaced by 203 · **path 3 — SIS.Api's own store — and it is already paid for.** 208 put the web on spec 301's orchestration, whose **write-ahead journal row already carries the whole request**: `IntegrationAttemptLog.StartAsync` inserts `RequestJson = SerializeObject(request)` into `PosIntegrationAttempt` on a fresh connection **before** the payer is called, `StringMaxType` (202's attachments ride inside), findable by `SubmissionReference == authId` which 301 stamps on **any** response carrying an id, refusals included. **No new table, column or write.** **Path 1 is richer than suspected and is the free fallback** — `auth/AuthResponse/{id}` returns **`AuthHeaderDto`**, eagerly fetching `AuthLines` + `AuthSupportingInfos`: all nine deductible rates, policy, every header flag, per-line qty/days-supply/selection-reason/diagnosis-index/deductible-group, attachments as base64. Two gaps: **`MaxCoverage` is on `NAuthLine` but missing from `AuthLineDto`** (one of 205's five agent inputs — priced, not taken) and **`NAuthDiagnosis` is dead code** (entity present, both DTOs commented out, never written) so diagnoses round-trip as the header `Diagnosis` string + per-line `Diagnosis`/`DiagnosisIndex`. **Path 2 dropped as a prefill seam** — `auth/AuthJson/{id}` exists and **WPF already calls it**, straight into a raw-text viewer (`NphiesAuthListController.cs:487`); nothing anywhere parses FHIR back into a form, and writing that parser recovers a payload we hold twice in flat form. **A reopen is a replay, not a restore** — 208 banned drafts and the row is terminal `SUBMITTED`, so it opens a **fresh** session and replays through existing verbs (**no new session verb**), reporting what did not come back rather than restoring silently. **~1.5 developer-days, zero Nphies-service change.** 🚩 **Corrects 203 on `Failed`**: NPHIES validation is only *one* of two sources — the service's own guards (unknown item `:402`, no Nphies category `:514`, unconfigured provider `:576`/payer `:581`, prescription ref >40 `:219`) throw before the POST and produce an identical `Failed`, *more* fixable in the form; but lines are built at `:562`, **after** them, so those leave a header-only row path 1 can't prefill — the second reason path 3 wins. 🚩 **The list must send `showAll=true`** (`AuthService.cs:1377` filters `!c.Error`) or refused rows never appear · [findings](assets/196-nphies/reopening-a-refused-request.md)

## Nphies build — spec 209

Building spec [209](209-nphies-web-spec.md) out of map [196](196-nphies-to-web-map.md). Twelve
vertical slices; **all twelve (210–221) are done** — the client track of spec 209 is complete and
waiting on SIS.Api — 217 was the core, the largest server term, and what these queue behind. ⚠ **Eleven of the twelve depend on SIS.Api work** — exactly
ONE endpoint ships today (`POST Nphies/CheckEligibility`, API key, **no grant filter**) and
`NpihesHttpService` has three methods. ✅ **The server set now exists too: BackOffice
[912](C:\Work\DMSCO\BackOffice\.issues\912-nphies-web-door.md)–[922](C:\Work\DMSCO\BackOffice\.issues\922-nphies-conformance-fixtures.md)**,
eleven tickets, frontier 912, each citing the contract by section — so 210's the frontier here and
912's the frontier there, and they can start the same morning. ✅ **The contract:
[Nphies web API v1.0](assets/209-nphies-contract/CONTRACT.md)** — frozen 2026-08-01, 16 passthrough
endpoints + 11 session verbs, the session-state projection and its ordering rule, the 19-field line
ownership table, a three-way error taxonomy with codes, and 12 conformance fixtures; every field name
read from the service source, not inferred. **The server tickets cite it by section.** Blocking edges
below are in-repo only; each ticket names its server dependency under Boundaries.

- [210](210-core-owns-the-latest-state-guard.md) — `core` owns the latest-state guard, and the console still drives an order unchanged · **done** · spec 209 · blocked by: — · **prefactor, the only ticket startable today** · the version/etag/contract-version guard moved out of `features/callcenter/console` into `core/engine-session/` with its suite, because 217 is the second consumer and features may not import features. **Zero behaviour change** — assertions unchanged, `applyState` generic over `{version, etag}` so 217 need not widen `core/`. Open q **answered: `session-fault.ts` travelled** — the Nphies contract §6 names the same two codes with the same three closed reasons. 814 tests green, boundary gate clean, `callcenter-drive.mjs` 508/508
- [211](211-an-eligibility-check-answers-in-two-axes.md) — A granted agent runs an eligibility check and gets an answer in two axes · **done 2026-08-02** · spec 209 · blocked by: — · **SLICE 0, the tracer** · the whole spine landed — new `features/nphies/` area, `eligibility` namespace, `/nphies/eligibility/new`, a top-level **Nphies** nav group whose leaf **fails closed** on the area's one `Nphies/Access` probe (in `core/`, since both features share the single grant), and the check act through `core/api`. Provider is a free per-act pick with **no default and no memory** — and **Fill deliberately does not choose one** though the last check names one. Both axes render, with the site qualifier **inside** the verdict badge ("Eligible · outside network") and the verdict **blank** on a non-Complete request even when the row carries `isEligible:true`. 🚩 **the two-axis module landed in `core/nphies/status.ts`, not in the feature** — 214 is a second feature and features may not import features, so 212 takes it as-is. 🚩 **contract gap logged:** §5 names `NAuth`'s columns; an eligibility derives Request from `Outcome`/`Success` and is **never `Cancelled`**. 🚩 **`success:false` outranks `outcome`** — the service fills `Outcome` before the exchange's validation errors throw, so a refusal can arrive saying `complete`; 214 owes the same ordering. 🚩 **law 10's `contractVersion` deliberately neither sent nor checked** — §1.1/§3.1 make this a passthrough of a DTO that has no such field, so the gap is named for a §8 revision rather than invented; 217 needs the answer. 849 tests green, drive **41/41** against mocked envelopes (SIS.Api down, all four server deps unbuilt), lint + build clean · ten decisions in `.afk/HITL-211.md`
- [212](212-the-eligibility-list-opens-on-a-visible-window.md) — The eligibility list opens on the last 7 days and says so with a removable chip · **done 2026-08-02** · spec 209 · blocked by: 211 · `/nphies/eligibility` is now the area's landing route. The chip states the window **it is actually showing** — a widened one stops calling itself "last 7 days" — and its ✕ **drops** the window rather than substituting a wider one. Both axes render through 211's `core/nphies/status`, unchanged for 214. 🚩 **`NEligibility` has no `Outcome` column** — the axis source is never persisted — so a stored row reads through a **second entry point** (`deriveStoredEligibilityAxes`: `success:false → Failed`, else `Complete`); the first cut flipped the *shared* default instead and that was **wrong**, because `FillResponse` fills `Outcome` only when the bundle carried a response resource while `Success = true` is unconditional after it. **`Pending` is unreachable on a stored row and the filter does not offer it.** 🚩 **the preauth-reference filter is NOT built** — §3.3 puts `preAuthRef` on the auth list only and an eligibility has no such reference; a ticket/spec correction, not an omission. 🚩 **two more named gaps:** the two axes ship as `request=`/`verdict=` which §3.3 does not list, and a removed chip's absent `fromDate` **must not** fall through to upstream's 3-day default. `showAll=true` matters more here than on the auth list. 874 tests green, drive **81/81** against mocked envelopes (SIS.Api down, the re-modelled list unbuilt), lint + build clean · fourteen decisions in `.afk/HITL-212.md`
- [213](213-two-coverages-force-a-pick.md) — A patient with two coverages must pick one before an authorization can be raised · **done 2026-08-02** · spec 209 · blocked by: 212 · `/nphies/eligibility/:id` — a **route, not a modal**, reached from a real `Open` **anchor** on the list because story 9 asks for a response that can be *linked to*. Every coverage listed; **one auto-selects — expired included** (the Verdict column is what says so, and the rule is keyed on the **count**, never on in-force); two or more force a pick with **all radios unchecked**. 🚩 **the pick token is the coverage's index, the seam carries the member id** — `MemberId` is nullable and not guaranteed unique across one patient's policies. 🚩 **an out-of-range pick is NO pick, never the first row** — a clamp is the quiet way this ticket fails. 🚩 **a lone coverage with a blank member id is still selected and still cannot be raised**, because §7.1's `Open` takes one; likewise no-coverages, each naming its own reason on a withheld-but-focusable act. 🚩 **the verdict is deliberately NOT a gate** — a not-in-force check can still raise. Seam is spec §1's URL verbatim, `?from=&coverage=`, behind a named `RAISE_AUTHORIZATION_ROUTE` so a moved route fails a test rather than an agent; 217 lands the other end. `CoverageList` + `formatStamp` extracted rather than copied. **No contract gap found** — §1.1 #4 answers the same DTO §3.1 does, so the detail shares the model. 888 tests green, drive **108/108** against mocked envelopes (SIS.Api down, the by-id read unbuilt), lint + build clean · eight decisions in `.afk/HITL-213.md`
- [214](214-an-authorization-row-states-both-facts.md) — An authorization row states whether we got an answer and what the payer said · **done 2026-08-02** · spec 209 · blocked by: 212 · `/nphies/authorizations` — the area's second feature + namespace, on 211's `core/nphies/status` **unchanged**, with only the Verdict's value set differing. The two **markers** are their own derivation (`authRowMarkers`), unreachable from the axes: a payer query renders on a row that is already `Complete` **with** a verdict, which is exactly why it cannot be a value of either. **Nothing asserts "ready to dispense"** — asserted by `Object.keys`, not by comment. 🚩 **`showAll=true` on every read** — the refused row is visibly on the list in the drive, and there is no state that wants it gone, so nothing can be left off. 🚩 **`Cancelled` outranks a stored `Complete`** (a cancel happens *after* an answer, so the row still carries an approval) and 🚩 **an unknown `AdjudicationOutcome` reads blank, never a nearby value**. **No `refetchInterval` anywhere** — manual Refresh with the load time beside it, and the drive idles 3.5 s to prove no poll. **No row links to a detail:** 216's route does not exist and a dead link is worse than a missing one. Four things graduated to `core/` because a feature may not import a feature — the **window + pager arithmetic**, `formatStamp`, the **providers lookup** (unchanged key, still one call for three screens) and, at review, the duplicated **`ListPager`**; the chip's markup stays per-namespace on purpose. 🚩 **gaps named:** no patient name on `AuthForListDto`; the two axes still absent from §3.3; a removed chip must not fall through to upstream's 3-day default (212's trap, other half); `preAuthRef` matches by equality. 921 tests green, drive **63/63** against mocked envelopes (SIS.Api down, the re-modelled list unbuilt), eligibility drive re-run **108/108**, lint + build clean · twelve decisions in `.afk/HITL-214.md`
- [215](215-a-row-offers-only-the-acts-its-state-permits.md) — A pending authorization can be status-checked or retried, and a completed undispensed one cancelled · **done 2026-08-02** · spec 209 · blocked by: 214 · `row-acts.ts` lands in the **feature** (216's detail is the same feature; `core/nphies/status` is shared only because two *features* render the axes). **All four acts on every row**, each offered or withheld with one of **seven cause-scoped reasons** — "unavailable" three times on one row teaches nothing — and the dispensed marker is read **before** the verdict, since a dispensed row is `Complete` with a good verdict, exactly the shape Cancel is offered on. 🚩 **the correction of record is asserted twice**: `Failed` offers no Retry, and its reason names **the payload**, not the state. 🚩 **the bodies are the contract's minus everything the server stamps** — read from the Nphies service source AND the parallel SIS.Api slice (BackOffice 916, on disk): `{reference}` · `{referenceId}` · `{reference, reasonCode, nullify:false, providerCode}`, with **no `referenceType`/`storeCode`/`staffId`/`claimType`** leaving the browser and the drive asserting the *absences* against the captured POST bodies. `reference` is the auth **id** (`CancellationService.cs:108`), and `nullify:false` is explicit because SIS.Api forwards it as asked. 🚩 **two contract gaps named:** §3.6 gives `reasonCode` no value set — so the cancel dialog **fetches** it (`CodeSystem?valueSet=TaskReasonCode`, the coding that reaches NPHIES) and defaults nothing; and §3.8 does not freeze the lookups' envelope, so `{contractVersion, items}` and a bare array are unwrapped in **one** place, because reading one fails as an **empty picker with no error** (214's `providers()` corrected here). 🚩 **a status check answering `success:false` is DATA** — the upstream sets `Success` only on a `Completed` task, so "still working" is this act's ordinary answer. **Two review findings fixed, both about where a fact is readable:** the in-flight state lives **above the grid** (a busy flag in the cell travels through `columnDefs`, and AG Grid's rebuild measurably threw keyboard focus to `<body>` mid-act — the drive now asserts focus survives), and a refused **cancellation** renders **inside** the dialog, because `showModal()`'s top layer paints any toast behind it under the scrim. 934 tests green, drive **94/94**, eligibility drive re-run 108/108, lint + build clean · fourteen decisions in `.afk/HITL-215.md`
- [216](216-the-detail-shows-the-payers-reason-in-words.md) — The authorization detail shows the payer's reason per line, in words · **done 2026-08-02** · spec 209 · blocked by: 214 · `/nphies/authorizations/:id`, reached from a real `Open` **anchor** on the list mirroring 213's — 214 shipped no link because this route did not exist, and it does now. **There is no rejection view to build:** the ordinary detail always carries per-line verdict / approved quantity / rejected / the decoded `BenefitReason`, so the **partial** the brief forgets — an *approved* header over a refused line — is just the ordinary detail read carefully, and the screen counts the refused lines rather than leaving an agent to scan for them. 🚩 **the dual-meaning field is read in ONE branch, and the branch is the Request state** — `failureMessage` is the feature's only reader, it answers `null` on `Complete` and on `Cancelled` whatever the field holds, and the drive proves it with a `Complete + Rejected` fixture whose `ErrorMessageShort` carries the *adjudication display* (`ProcessAuthResponse.cs:120`'s branch) and never reaches the screen. 🚩 **the blank-until-Complete rule is applied to the LINES too** — `ProcessAuthResponse` writes header and lines from one response, so a `Failed`/`Pending` line still carries `approved` and rendering it would publish a verdict the payer never gave. 🚩 **no dispensed marker on the detail** — `IsDispensed` is on `AuthForListDto` and **absent** from `AuthHeaderDto`, so showing one would mean inventing a field; the payer-query marker *is* on the header DTO and renders. **Attachments cost nothing** — the response already carries the base64, an image renders inline and a PDF is a link, **no modal**, and `days-supply` is filtered out by the *payload* rather than by a category spelling the contract does not freeze. 🚩 **contract gap named:** §3.5 spells the submit field `contentType`, but what comes back is the service's two-valued `attachmentType` (`image`|`pdf`, `ProcessAddAuthRequest.cs:216` / `Extensions.cs:725`) — the client mirrors the service's own map exactly and passes a real MIME through, for the §8 revision. **No money is computed anywhere** — no sum, no total, one server field per cell (law 1); the only derived number on the screen is a count of refused lines. Field names read from **SIS.Api's own** `NphiesAuthHeaderDto`/`NphiesAuthLineDto`/`NphiesAuthSupportingInfoDto` (BackOffice 916 is done and on disk), which is what the browser actually receives. 953 tests green (19 new), drive **121/121** against mocked envelopes (SIS.Api down), lint + build clean · nine decisions in `.afk/HITL-216.md`
- [217](217-a-live-engine-session.md) — An agent builds a request in a live engine session, and a duplicate item is refused as it is added · **done 2026-08-02** · spec 209 · blocked by: 210, 213 · **THE CORE, and it is a session rather than a form.** `/nphies/authorizations/new?from=&coverage=` opens a real engine transaction on mount and abandons it on the way out — six of the eleven verbs (open · state · addItem · changeQty · voidLine · abandon), every mutating one answering the whole state, no reducer and no delta. 🚩 **the eligibility is NOT fetched here** — §2 has `reference` fetched from it *at Open*, so identity arrives inside the state, which also settles the boundary (that read belongs to the other feature). 🚩 **the duplicate is refused twice over and reads identically both times**: `refuseAdd` states it forward with **no round trip** and names the **quantity control**, `readAddRefusal` turns the door's own `ITEM_ALREADY_ON_REQUEST` into the same shape for a screen that has fallen behind — and **a voided line does not block its own item**, or the named remedy would point at a line no longer on the request. 🚩 **a voided line is drawn, struck through and inert**, and the heading counts what the payer is asked for rather than what is on screen. 🚩 **leaving is intercepted whenever a transaction is open** — warned inline (no modal anywhere) with lines, abandoned silently without them, because an OPEN transaction left for the sweeper is the litter this design retired; `beforeunload` covers the closed tab and **the login bounce is exempt**, since 401 is `core/api`'s. 🚩 **the session will not open until the plant is resolved** (law 8) and the shell's store switcher is held still by a new `core/engine-session/store-lock` ⚠ that switcher is mounted nowhere today, so that half is latent. Two more graduations to `core/` for 210's reason: **`newRequestId`** (re-exported from the console's api.ts, no call site moved) and the first real use of **`session-fault`** — `SESSION_CLOSED`/`NOT_YOUR_SESSION` stop the form and clear the state rather than banner over a request that is gone; **`SESSION_BUSY`'s bounded retry is deliberately deferred** to the ticket that adds concurrent verbs. 🚩 **contract gap named:** §1.2 says item search "reuses the existing item lookup" and names no route — the only one this repo has is another feature's behind another grant — so the add-row is an **item-number field, not a typeahead**. 980 tests green (27 new), drive **61/61** against mocked envelopes (SIS.Api down, all six verbs unbuilt), the three earlier drives re-run 121/121 · 108/108 · call-centre 508/508, lint + build clean · eleven decisions in `.afk/HITL-217.md`
- [218](218-five-money-inputs-and-nothing-else.md) — The agent's five money inputs reach the request, and nothing else is editable · **done 2026-08-02** · spec 209 · blocked by: 217 · the three insurance verbs land (`setInsurance` · `updateLineInsurance` · `updateLineMeta`), taking the session to **nine of eleven**. Editable: the header rate block (three groups of rate + cap + **paid-outside**, all nine sent whole because §1.2's body is `{g1,g2,g3}`), line qty, line Max Coverage, line Days Supply, + Selection Reason. Everything else is a **value, not a disabled control** — the drive walks columns 4–11 of a row asserting not one holds an input, which is a claim about the whole surface rather than the fields somebody remembered. 🚩 **the editability map is the assertion**: `REQUEST_FIELDS` names every field the form draws and `AGENT_EDITABLE_FIELDS` is exactly seven of them (five inputs — the rate block counts two boxes — plus the code), so a new projection field is a *decision* rather than an oversight. 🚩 **Days Supply 1–100 AT THE CELL** ⇒ WPF's three ranges collapse to one and its submit-time sweep + dialog are **deleted, not ported**; the door's `DAYS_SUPPLY_INVALID` is the backstop and the drive proves nothing ever asks. 🚩 **a zero cap WARNS, and the rule turned out to be about the keystroke, not the value**: the engine lands `maxCoverage: 0` on every line, so the first cut read a typed `0` over a stored `0` as "unchanged" and said nothing — which is the exact silent no-op §4 exists for; it now warns whenever the agent **types** one and stays quiet on an untouched cell. 🚩 **Selection Reason: the projection's `selectionReasonEditable` flag is the authority, the `Generic` category rule is the same rule kept for a door that has not filled it in** — and `NonMed` and `Brand-IR` both keep the picker, the second **deliberately**, because the service overwrites it at submit and someone "fixing" that would change what reaches the payer. Reasons are **fetched** (`CodeSystem?valueSet=SelectionReason`), blocked codes dropped except one the line already holds. 🚩 **the cap re-buckets siblings** — per-group caps share a pool — so the stubbed engine in the drive re-prices *every* line from the header terms and pools per bucket, which is what makes both flow claims observable rather than commented. 🚩 **gap named:** §4 puts no upper bound on a rate, so 0–100 is this client's and is logged for a §8 ruling. 1002 tests green (22 new), drive **92/92** against stubbed envelopes (SIS.Api down, all three verbs and the paid-outside column unbuilt), the two earlier Nphies drives re-run 121/121 · 108/108, lint + build clean · twelve decisions in `.afk/HITL-218.md`
- [219](219-supporting-material-diagnoses-and-attachments.md) — Morphology appears with its cause, and a 6 MB photo becomes a 250 KB attachment · **done 2026-08-02** · spec 209 · blocked by: 217 · `setHeader` lands, taking the session to **ten of eleven** — only `submit` is left, and it is 220's. **Principal is a radio**, so uniqueness is a property of the control: `choosePrincipal` leaves exactly one and demotes the row that lost it to `secondary` rather than dropping the diagnosis. 🚩 **"a neoplasm" is the SERVICE's answer, not a code range in the browser** — `isNeedMorph` off the diagnosis lookup row (`NDiagnosis.IsNeedMorph`), fetched for the principal's own code, so an ICD range spelled here could not disagree with the table the exchange validates against. The morphology field **appears with its cause and leaves with it**, carrying a *required because C50.9 — …* heading, and 🚩 **the value goes with it**: a row that stops being principal loses its morphology rather than riding one to the payer. 🚩 **both lookups are SEARCHES, not catalogues** — `CoreService.GetDiagnosesAsync` answers `[]` for an empty query and `Take(500)` otherwise, so the row types (2 chars, 250 ms) and the client caches no ICD catalogue; shapes read from the service's own `DiagnosisModel`/`MorphModel`. Exception prescription is one checkbox, sent alone. Attachments: canvas-downscale to 2000 px/q0.85 (a 3 MB photo → 679 KB in the drive), PDFs untouched and **refused over 5 MB at the picker**, **the type dropdown dissolves** (MIME derives `contentType`, and every image becomes a JPEG — which makes the service's hardcoded `image/jpeg` true for the web's traffic), **closed 7-value title select with duplicates ALLOWED** and a test that says so out loud so nobody "fixes" it by analogy with the duplicate *item*. Mandatory-at-least-one is a **form state** — banner + Submit disabled + a blocker that names itself — and the preview is an inline lightbox off **the same data URL that will be sent** (asserted `===` the row's thumbnail, no `<dialog>` anywhere). 🚩 **the attachments never touch the projection**: the engine has never heard of them, they ride on `submit`, so a state read cannot clear what the agent attached. ⚠ **the Submit button carries the gate, not the act** — 219 owns which blockers hold (`noPrincipal` · `morphologyMissing` · `noAttachment`) and 220 owns the clinical-edit check and the submission; wiring a partial submit would send a request without the gate §3.5 puts before it. 1028 tests green (26 new), drive **122/122** against stubbed envelopes (SIS.Api down, `setHeader` and both lookups unbuilt), the two earlier Nphies drives re-run 121/121 · 108/108, lint + build clean · twelve decisions in `.afk/HITL-219.md`. **server dep:** diagnosis + morphology lookups only — attachments have **zero**
- [220](220-a-refused-submit-keeps-the-agent-on-the-form.md) — A refused submit keeps the agent on the form, with the reasons on the rows that caused them · **done 2026-08-02** · spec 209 · blocked by: 218, 219 · `submit` lands, closing §1.2's table at **eleven of eleven**. Submit is a **gated path, not a button**: `submit-gate.ts` states every reason it is unavailable **in one place** — no items (voided lines are not items) · no attachment · no principal · morphology unset · no coverage · no provider — **each naming itself**, with the server's own `submitBlockers` joining the same list in its own words and a server code the client already owns suppressed 🚩 **unconditionally** (`NO_ATTACHMENTS` cannot be the engine's to say: attachments exist only inside the submit body). Clinical edit is **one surface in two shapes**, inline (no modal, per the spec's own ruling — logged): the fatal shape is the warning shape with the confirm button **removed, not disabled**, and 🚩 **fatal is exactly `F`** because that is exactly what `ValidateClinicalEditForAuth` treats as fatal — an unrecognised type is a warning here for the same reason it is one there. Its request/result shapes are **not in the contract** and were read from the service's `ClinicalEditValidator.cs:285,299,319`, not invented. 🚩 **`Failed` = refused before the payer saw it** ⇒ the agent **stays on the form**, each reason on the row that caused it and nothing dropped: a `lineId: null` — the header-only case where the guards threw before the lines were built — *and* a reason naming a line the request does not hold both land on the header. 🚩 **A timeout is IN FLIGHT, never failed**: `SubmitNextAct` has no resubmit member, `submitIsLocked` is permanent, Submit is **gone rather than disabled**, and 🚩 **the exit abandons NOTHING** — the leave interception is exempted while locked, because the one act the screen offers would otherwise be answered with a *Discard* button for a request that may already be at the exchange. An accepted submit **navigates to the authorization it created**. Whole form frozen for the submit leg. 1055 tests green (27 new), drive **159/159** (scenarios 31–38) against a stubbed engine — SIS.Api down, nothing on this door built — lint + build clean · nine decisions in `.afk/HITL-220.md`, review findings in `.afk/REVIEW-220.md`. **server dep:** clinical-edit validate · submit (the explicit 100 s leg, whose timeout stays the **server's**: the browser sets none) · assumes the zero-price guard
- [221](221-reopening-replays-and-reports.md) — Reopening a refused request replays it, and reports what did not come back · **done 2026-08-02** · spec 209 · blocked by: 220, 216 · closes the wave. **A replay, not a restore** — `?copyOf=<authId>` on the existing form route opens a **fresh** session and replays through six verbs that already exist (`Open` · `SetInsurance` · `SetHeader` · `AddItem` · `UpdateLineInsurance` · `UpdateLineMeta`), **no new session verb**, asserted as data against §1.2's eleven so a later edit cannot quietly add one. 🚩 **The report IS the feature**: the plan is compared against the **engine's own state** after a settling `State` read, never against "the verbs returned 200" — a verb that answered and landed a line at a new price is exactly the case a success-counting replay calls clean, and it is the one that has the agent resubmit a quietly different request. Named: `refused` (the door's own sentence) · `missing` · `repriced` (both figures) · `recategorised` (an item that **lost** its Nphies category) · `quantityDiffers` · `capNotApplied` · `metaNotApplied` · 🚩 `notPricedYet`, because reading an unsettled price as "unchanged" is a silent restore by another route. Prefills from the **write-ahead journal row** — shape read from the service's own `AuthRequest`/`AuthItemRequest`/`AuthSupportingInfoRequest`, **not invented**, since §3.9 freezes the endpoint and no response shape — which still carries the **per-line cap `AuthLineDto` drops** and still prefills a **header-only** refusal (terms, policy, diagnoses intact; `noLinesRecorded` said out loud). 🚩 Diagnoses decoded as `type|code` per `NphiesDiagnosis.GetDiagnosisList`, morphology off the `morphology` supporting info's **`code`** — both read from source; an unreadable row is dropped **and reported**. 🚩 **Attachments deliberately NOT replayed and reported as such**: the row stores `image`/`pdf`, not a MIME, so rebuilding §3.5's `contentType` would mean guessing one at a national exchange. 215's `reopenNotWiredYet` retired — `openRefusal` is live on `Failed` and offered from the detail's failure block too. 🚩 **The free fallback is built too**: an authorization raised at a **till** never went through the orchestration that writes the journal, so on `AUTH_NOT_FOUND` — *and only that code*, because a dead wire means the row may well exist and the thinner source would silently drop the caps — the plan is built from `AuthResponse/{id}` (216's own read, no new endpoint) and carries §3.4's known gap as `capNotRecorded`: **reported, never worked around, and no cap invented for any line**. 1081 tests green (26 new), drive **192/192** (scenarios 39–44) against a stubbed journal, lint + build clean · twelve decisions in `.afk/HITL-221.md`, both review axes in `.afk/REVIEW-221.md`. ⚠ the drive was **red at HEAD** (a `.check()` racing a controlled checkbox); verified, fixed in the harness, logged. **server dep:** one journal-row read — **no new table, column or write, and no Nphies-service change**

## A Loy member, read-only, in the portal — wayfinder map 222

Charted 2026-08-05. The WPF `Sartawi.Retail/IC` section comes to the web, but only its lookup half:
one field resolves a member by mobile **or** LoyId, and the screen shows general information plus
three tabs — Activities, Sales, Actions. **Read-only by ruling** — no act on the screen in phase 1.
Destination is a **ready spec**. Landing in a new `features/loy/` area under `/loy/*`. ✅ **The
server side already exists**: `Loy/Member/{loyId}`, `Loy/MemberByMobile/{mobile}`, and the three
`Loy/Reports/*` reads all ship in `SIS.Api/Endpoints/Loy/LoyEndpoints.cs` — but 🚩 **they are shut to
the browser**: 224 found 802's default-deny cookie branch answers a portal call with a bare 403, so
a gated `*Web/*` door, an `Access` probe and a new `BackOfficeScreen[LoyMember,03]` are needed after
all. ✅ 228 settled that **the client does not wait**: a `LoyWeb/*` door is a BackOffice issue the
spec cites, and oms-react builds against **mocked envelopes from 223's inventory** — so 🚩 **no
ticket here may be called done on a live call**, and the tab-less "general information only" slice
is **dead**. ⚠ WPF's Sales tab has no
endpoint behind it (it queries `RetailTrxDetail` through NHibernate); the web takes what
`Loy/Reports/LoyaltySales` gives, lets the columns differ, and **creates no endpoint for it**.
🚩 **WPF is a source, not a target** — divergence is the user's ruling, not a concession: IC supplies
the data and the domain rules, never the layout or the interaction, and "because that's what WPF
does" is not an answer any ticket on this map may give. ✅ **Frontier empty as of 2026-08-06** — all
eight tickets (223–230) are resolved. [The shape of the member screen](227-the-shape-of-the-member-screen.md)
closed last and took **variant B**: the field collapses into an identity bar carrying the searched key
only, the member lives at **`/loy/members/:loyId`** (which also **closed the URL/deep-linking fog
item**), the header is not a tab and not sticky, one headline number, one shut disclosure, Activities
lands and only Actions carries a count. ✅ **The last scope question is ruled** (2026-08-06): WPF's
**Activity Summary** grid and its endpoint-less Old Account (Mobile) field are **out of scope —
"obsolete"** on the user's call, so the destination stands at general information plus three tabs. The
phase-2 seam stays deliberately dim (a read-only phase 1 has nothing to attach). **Map complete →
`/to-spec`**, which also mints the BackOffice `LoyWeb/*` door issue per 228's six-item spec **plus 230's
two amendments** (map `MemberType` through, drop the `LOY-00101` archived refusal).

- [232](232-the-pager-graduates-to-core.md) — The pager graduates to core and can page by a real total · **done** · spec 231 · blocked by: — · **prefactor, done first** · 🚩 four verified drifts against spec 231's "reuse the existing `GridPager`": it lives in `features/admin/ua-admin/` so a `features/loy/*` import **fails `npm run lint`**'s boundary gate (`check-boundaries.mjs` discovers features from the tree); `PAGE_SIZE` is hardcoded **50** where Loy Actions pages **25**; `pagerButtonEnablement` drives Next off `isCapped` ("more rows beyond THIS page", a Ua-users envelope flag) where Loy actions returns a **real `recordsCount`**, so Next must come from `page < pageCount`; and the component hardcodes `useTranslation('ua-admin')`. Move component + pure arithmetic to `@/core/*`, parameterize page size and namespace, **add** a next-from-total path beside next-from-capped (both must survive), repoint the single consumer `UaAdminUsersPage`. Ua Users observably unchanged · **landed**: `@/core/ui/pager.ts` + `@/core/ui/GridPager.tsx`, `pager.*` keys in `common.json`, Ua Users' own `PAGE_SIZE` in `features/admin/ua-admin/page-size.ts`; 20 pure cases, drive re-run with every pager assertion green
- [233](233-one-field-resolves-a-member.md) — One field resolves a member, and a shut door never reads as "no member matches" · **done 2026-08-06** · spec 231 · blocked by: — · **SLICE 0 — the tracer bullet** · the whole spine thin: new `features/loy/` area, `core/models/loy.ts`, the two member reads, the `resolveMember` cascade, `/loy/members` → `/loy/members/:loyId`, the identity bar carrying **the searched key only** with Change (reopen pre-filled) / New lookup, a header of name + Loy ID + mobile, the `loy` namespace registered. 🚩 **The cascade condition is the whole ticket**: only `LOY-00100` retries as a LoyId — 224's ungranted call is a **bare 403**, so cascade-on-anything would render the shut door *and every outage* as "no member matches", doubling load on a dead API while telling the agent to re-read a correct number. Field **compacts to digits only** (no dialling code, no `PadLeft` — key-building is server-side); box never rewritten; double miss is a neutral **client** sentence (knowingly bending api-envelope, since each server sentence names only one of two things tried); explicit submit, blank silent; `LOY-00101` kept as a defensive guard. 🚩 **URL holds the LoyId, never what was typed**, so a refresh re-reads by key and does not replay the cascade. **No menu item yet** — URL-only, so the screen never exists in the nav ungated. 🚩 Verified while slicing: **`formatStamp` does not exist** (it is `formatDateTime`/`formatShortDate`), and `isBlankDate` already handles the `0001-01-01` sentinel and is exported for exactly this reuse · **landed**: `core/models/loy.ts` (spec §6's `blockedReason` code/description split as two types, mapped at the api boundary), `features/loy/member/{api,resolve-member,MemberLookupPage}`, the `loy` namespace, both routes, and **`apiErrorKind` added to `core/api.ts`** beside `apiErrorCode` so the cascade names the taxonomy arm instead of re-implementing it. 12 pure cases (403 / 500 / network / other-business each propagate on call one and never reach call two), `tools/loy-member-drive.mjs` **34/34**, typecheck + lint + build green. Two copy calls made against 227's prototype and logged in `.afk/HITL-233.md`: 🚩 the hint **no longer publishes the cascade** (story 3 wants the ordering invisible, and "the server does" was wrong — the client sequences the calls), and **Change grew a Cancel** because reopening the field hides the bar that owns New lookup. 🚩 Nothing driven against a live SIS.Api — the `LoyWeb` door is BackOffice 977–979
- [234](234-the-loyalty-nav-appears-only-for-a-granted-session.md) — The Loyalty nav group appears only for a granted session, and fails closed · **done 2026-08-06** · spec 231 · blocked by: 233 · new top-level nav group under `/loy/*`, one `LoyWeb/Access` probe on **one** query key shared by the menu item's `accessProbe` and the page's in-page guard (the established `omsAccessApi`/`uaAdminApi`/`sessionMonitorApi` shape, so nav and screen can never disagree). 🚩 **Fails closed** — any error, unseeded grant, missing table or engine fault hides the item *and* denies the screen; the failure mode designed against is a PII surface left open because a probe threw and the client read the throw as "probably fine". Deep link by an ungranted user lands on the denied backstop · **landed**: `LoyAccessResult` in `core/models/loy.ts`, `LOY_ACCESS_KEY` + the `canOpenLoyMember` predicate exported from `features/loy/member/api.ts` and used **verbatim** by both consumers (sharing the predicate, not just the key, makes "nav and screen cannot disagree" structural); **Loyalty** group (`Gem`) → **Member lookup** (`UserSearch`); the in-page backstop on the Nphies wording. The probe stays **with its feature** — two consumers, both this feature's, which is the `uaAdminApi`/`sessionMonitorApi` shape; `core/` is for when a *second feature* needs it. 🚩 Three things the build settled: the guard needs the shell's own `staleTime: Infinity` + `retry: false` or the screen's mount **refetches the key the menu just filled** (the drive measured 2 calls, now asserts 1); a **bare 403 is a refusal, not an outage** — `core/api` classifies it `unknown`, not `auth`, so unbranched it told an agent to "try again in a moment" at a permanently shut door; and the member read is gated on the probe too (`enabled: !!loyId && allowed`) so an ungranted deep link fires **no PII read** on its way to the backstop. Pure: `features/loy/member/access.test.ts` (only `=== true` admits) + `layout/menu-loy.test.ts` (the **real** `MENU` through `resolveMenu`: denied/errored/malformed/pending each drop the group *and* its child). `tools/loy-member-drive.mjs` **47/47** (granted · denied · 500 · 403). Both reviews clean; the backstop card is now the third hand-rolled copy of the Nphies one — a `core/ui` candidate, logged not taken. 🚩 Nothing driven against a live SIS.Api (BackOffice 979 is the probe)
- [235](235-the-member-header-says-who-this-is-once.md) — The member header says who this is, and says it once · **done 2026-08-06** · spec 231 · blocked by: 233 · the three **independent** chips with no precedence rule (tier always · member type iff `memberType !== 'M'` · blocked iff `blockedReasonCode` set) — 🚩 an ordinary member gets **no status chip at all**, so a chip always means "read me" and an archived-and-blocked member never hides one fact behind the other; the points block with **one** headline figure (`pointsBalance`, SAR as its subline, Expiring the only tinted figure and only when non-zero); **one disclosure shut by default** over the field tail; `profile`/`accrualFactor`/`redemptionFactor`/`exchangeRate`/`pointsExpireSoonDays`/`profileUpdated` drawn **nowhere** (engine machinery, not member). Plus `codes.ts`: **a code is data unless its set is closed in server source** — `tier`, `activityStatus`, and `blockedReasonCode` (`CM`/`IA`, the named exception) earn keys; 🚩 `gender` **passes through** because it looks closed and isn't; an unknown value returns `null` so the caller renders the **bare code**, never `t(key, {defaultValue})` (same pixels, but the guard would live at each call site instead of in one tested module). A passed-through code is labelled **"City code"**, not "City" · **landed**: `features/loy/member/{codes,member-header}.ts` (both pure, 22 cases) + `MemberHeader.tsx`, a thin renderer holding no rule; chips reuse `core/ui/StatusBadge` by **`Severity`** (`warn`/`mute`/`bad`) so no call site can invent a colour and the contrast gate covers the pair. 🚩 **A fourth closed set earned a map — `memberType`**: the ticket's table omits it, but the chips need the *words* Archived/Non-loyalty/Family and 230 names `LoyMemberTypeConstants` closing all four, which is 229's own test passed; a raw `A` in a chip whose job is to say *archived* would have been the failure. A chip whose field is **absent** is suppressed rather than drawn empty (`tier` is nullable; `memberType` exists only via 230's mapping amendment). 🚩 Two drive assertions were asserting **nothing** until tightened — body-wide `!/0001/` passes trivially because the stub LoyId `100001293` contains it, and `\bX\b` never matches in a `textContent` with no word boundaries; both now read the element they mean. `tools/loy-member-drive.mjs` **67/67**, 1135 pure cases, typecheck + lint + build green. Money subline uses the app's single 2dp `formatMoney` (`561.00 SAR`) not the prototype's `561` — logged in `.afk/HITL-235.md`, revisit at 237's currency-aware formatter. 🚩 Nothing driven against a live SIS.Api

- [225](225-one-field-that-resolves-a-member.md) — One field that resolves a member · **done 2026-08-05** · map 222 · blocked by: 223 · grilling · **there is no discrimination rule — the field never classifies what was typed.** `resolveMember(typed)` calls `LoyWeb/MemberByMobile/{typed}` and, **only** on a `LOY-00100` business miss, `LoyWeb/Member/{typed}`; WPF's fallback survives inverted — it retried after guessing wrong, this retries *because* guessing goes stale the day the number range rolls over. Sequencing lives **client-side** in `features/loy/api.ts`, not a fifth door route: 228's door stays at four reads, and a pure module is the one thing provable with SIS.Api down. 🚩 The correctness crux is the cascade condition — 224's ungranted call returns a **bare 403**, so cascade-on-anything would render the shut door *and every outage* as "no member matches"; `auth`/`server`/`network` abort and show themselves. The field **compacts to digits** (whitespace, dashes, parens, leading `+`) and nothing else — no dialling code, no `PadLeft`, no country rule — because 🚩 `LoyMobileNumbers.Parse` does `.Trim().TrimStart('+')` and never strips internal separators, so a pasted `+966 55 500 0111` misses **server-side today**. The box is never rewritten (the header shows the payload's stored `Mobile`, which *is* the key); a double miss is a **neutral client sentence in an empty state**, knowingly bending api-envelope because the outcome is composed from two calls and each server sentence names only one thing tried; **explicit submit** only, blank is silent. 🚩 Two ticket questions the code answered outright: **ambiguity does not exist** — `GetMemberByMobile` is a `FirstOrDefault` and `UpdateMemberThatUseSameMobile` **blanks the previous holder's mobile** and blocks them `CM`, so WPF's commented-out family-lookup dialog is dead code and **no disambiguation UI is built**; and the try-both rule **cannot collide** — a LoyId is 9 pure digits (`100001293`, `NumberRange` zero-pad, no prefix) while `NormaliseTyped` always prepends a dialling code, so a typed LoyId is searched as a 12-digit key no real mobile can equal. `LOY-00101` survives only as a **defensive guard** — 230 dissolved the branch by dropping the archived refusal on the door — never as a no-match. Nothing driven against a live SIS.Api

Third source fact: `Amount`/`Qty` are the **signed** twins on a return but `UnitPrice` is **not**, so a return row reads `-1.00 · 12.00 · -12.00`. Volume: the capped tabs **always state the ceiling** ("Most recent 100 activities") + an at-cap "there may be older…" — a false positive at exactly-100 beats a false negative at 4,000, and a bare row count is never shown; Actions states its **real total** and pages **25** via the existing `GridPager`. Sort/filter ✓ on the two capped tabs, ✗ on Actions — *sort what you hold, never what you're paging through*, which is the Nphies rule applied where it actually binds. Fetch **lazy on first open** ⇒ only the open tab can be loading or failed; failure is an inline `ErrorBanner` + **Retry scoped to that tab** (earned: a heavy-member Sales SQL timeout is a **raw 500 with no envelope**), per-tab empty sentences not a shared "No data". **No row links** — checked: no route takes a TrxNumber/`ActivityId`/`ActionNo`, and `oms/document/:documentNo` is a different identifier space that would 404. **No export** — resolves the map's Export fog item: a capped list becomes a file that outlives its caveat. Left to 229 (the codes) and 227 (the layout); nothing driven against a live SIS.Api

## The collection documents come to the web — wayfinder map 240

Charted 2026-08-07. The WPF collection suite ports to oms-react, read-only, for the collection
supervisor and the accountant: four inquiry screens (Collection, ACR, Deposit, Collection Attempts)
plus the two printable documents they open — the سند قبض **collection receipt** and the **ACR**
follow-up form. **The breaker is exactness**: neither document is done until a side-by-side render
has been signed off against its WPF original. Settled at chart time — WPF POS keeps its own XAML
voucher (nothing shared as code), the C# builders move **server-side** so the two clients cannot
drift, the screens are read-only, and the facsimiles are a documented exception to the zero-literal
and logical-Tailwind rules. Splits into a frontend wave here and a smaller backend wave in
`C:\Work\DMSCO\BackOffice`, meant to run in parallel.

## An invoice receipt downloads from the web — spec 261 (tickets 262-266)

The frontend half of BackOffice map **984** / spec
[1042](file:///C:/Work/DMSCO/BackOffice/.issues/1042-headless-retail-invoice-render-rail-spec.md), whose
Out-of-Scope hands this repo the search form and "nothing more" (D13). **The server is already built and
live** — a resident `BackOffice.exe renderhost` on the loopback renders the Stimulsoft receipt (net472,
Windows-only, so IIS cannot), and SIS.Api fronts it with three cookie-gated routes. Those routes have no
caller; that is the whole effort. The wire contract is settled at
[`988-search-download-contract.md`](file:///C:/Work/DMSCO/BackOffice/.issues/assets/988-search-download-contract.md)
and its §2 TypeScript is pasted verbatim so the row shape cannot drift between the two repos.

- [261](261-retail-invoice-download-web-spec.md) — An invoice receipt downloads from the web — spec · **ready** · one screen in a **new `reports` area** (`/reports/invoice`), one required field, a candidate list, a PDF per row · sliced into 262–266 · ⚠ two hard constraints shape it: a `<a href>` download is **impossible** (SIS.Api's cookie branch demands the `X-Web-Client` CSRF header, which a browser navigation cannot send → 401), and `core/api.ts` **cannot fetch a non-JSON body** at all. Records all seven of the contract's §6 guesses and their disposition — 🚩 **§6.3 measured and substantively wrong**: `Store.Description` is `صيدلية الدواء <storecode>`, the company name plus the code (1508 distinct over 1540 stores), carrying no branch identity, so `storeName` is demoted to a secondary column rather than the wire being changed
- [262](262-the-api-client-learns-to-fetch-a-file.md) — The API client learns to fetch a file · **done** · *(spec 261)* · blocked by: — · **prefactor**, lands alone because it touches two SHIPPED features. 🔑 `api.blob()` — the whole ticket is one branch: **success and failure read different body types off the same response** (`res.blob()` vs the existing envelope→`ApiError` tail, refactored not duplicated). Plus `attemptId` on `HttpGeneralResponse` — ⚠ a **top-level sibling** of `message`/`errors`, not an entry inside `errors[]`. Plus `downloadCsv` graduates to `@/core/util/download-file.ts`, **pre-authorized by collection's own docblock** ("graduates to `@/core` … when a third consumer lands") — both existing call sites repointed in the same commit. ⚠ `credentials` stays `'same-origin'` against the contract's `'include'`: vite proxies `/api`, so it already is · **built**: `request<T>` split into `send` (base + credentials + CSRF header + `handle401`), `readEnvelope`, `failureFromEnvelope` and `businessFromEnvelope` — every arm shared with `requestBlob`, no second copy, and the ok-path's `body.statusCode` deliberately kept distinct from the failure paths' `|| res.status` so no existing caller shifts. Parsing is its own pure module, `core/util/content-disposition.ts`, quote-aware rather than `split(';')` (mutation-checked: the naive split fails exactly the `;`-inside-quotes case). 🚩 One addition beyond the letter of the ticket: a **2xx whose `Content-Type` says JSON** is mapped as a refusal instead of being saved to disk as "the PDF" — keyed on the content type so it can never fire on an `application/pdf` (`/standards-review` read it as scope creep; kept, and logged). `download-file.ts` carries no test that moved — **neither feature copy had one** — so a new suite was written at the new path, pinning the parked anchor and the **deferred** revoke. - [265](265-a-row-downloads-its-receipt.md) — A row downloads its receipt · **done** · *(spec 261)* · blocked by: 262, 264 · the point of the effort. 🔑 **503 and 504 must not be collapsed** — 503 is a host recycling and a retry works; 504 is a hung render and a watchdog about to kill it. ⚠ **No client retry**: SIS.Api already tried twice (250 ms, 1 s), so by the time a 503 arrives three attempts have failed. ⚠ The search returns **unrenderable rows on purpose** (cash clearances `700`, training, suspended — owner ruling 988), so anything not `Sales`/`Return` gets a **confirm**, never a filter and never a disabled action. `attemptId` on 422/504 only, copyable — it is the `ReportRenderAttempt` row id and **there is no separate audit**.
